Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on giants structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 372. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 250 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_372(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 372. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^250(S, F) is trivial. QED.
